```

- Start from good initial (binary from initial_h threshold or optimize continuous then binarize?). Need initial sequence. We can create from initial_h_values: sort by descending value take n/2 ones. But initial_h continuous might be good. Or find deterministic patterns, random balanced, etc.

Maybe loop over lags with slices is simpler and reliable. N=250 L=499. A gradient pass:
```
grad = np.zeros(N)
for l in range(L):
    w = weights[l]
    if w==0: continue
    k = l - (N-1)
    m = N - abs(k)
    if k>=0:
        grad[k:k+m] += w       # linear A for h_index p from k to N-1
        # derivative -h_{p-k}: for p=k..N-1, h[p-k] -> add -w * h[0:m] to grad[k:k+m]
        grad[k:k+m] -= w * h[:m]
        # derivative -h_{p+k}: for p=0..m-1, h[p+k] -> add -w * h[k:k+m] to grad[:m]
        grad[:m] -= w * h[k:k+m]
    else:
        r=-k
        m=N-r
        grad[:m] += w
        # -h_{p-k}: p=0..m-1? p-k=p+r, h[p+r] -> grad[:m]-=w*h[r:m+r]
        grad[:m] -= w * h[r:m+r]
        # -h_{p+k}: p=r..N-1? h[p-k? wait k=-r, p+k=p-r valid p>=r -> h[p-r], for p=r..N-1; grad[r:]-=w*h[:m]
        grad[r:] -= w * h[:m]
```

Let's think about continuous SLSQP performance and correctness with analytic gradient.
Implement weighted_grad efficiently:
```
def weighted_grad(w, h, n):
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    L = 2*n-1
    # precomputed ranges? Function cannot use closures; can have top-level `_RANGES` cache? Maybe just loop.
    c = n-1
    for idx in range(L):
        wi = w[idx]
        if wi == 0.0: continue
        k = idx - c
        if k >= 0:
            if k == 0:
                m = n
                grad += wi
                grad -= wi*(2.0*h)
                continue
            m = n-k
            grad[k:k+m] += wi
            grad[k:k+m] -= wi*h[:m]
            grad[:m] -= wi*h[k:k+m]
        else:
            r = -k
            m = n-r
            grad[:m] += wi
            grad[:m] -= wi*h[r:m+r]
            grad[r:] -= wi*h[:m]
    return grad
```
Need for k=0 formula `grad += wi; grad -= wi*h; grad -= wi*h` works with above general? For k>=0,m=n, grad[0:n]+=w; grad[0:n]-=w*h; grad[0:n]-=w*h good. But in general k=0 the second term h[:m] and third h[k:k+m] same. Good. We can use code without special:
```
if k>=0:
  m=n-k
  grad[k:k+m] += w
  grad[k:k+m] -= w*h[:m]
  grad[:m] -= w*h[k:k+m]
else:
  r=-k; m=n-r
  grad[:m] += w
  grad[:m] -= w*h[r:m+r]
  grad[r:] -= w*h[:m]
```
Check for k>0: derivative terms:
A_k positions p=k..N-1 linear +w.
-p-k: p=k..N-1, h[p-k]=h[0..m-1] -> grad[k:]-=w*h[:m]
-p+k: p=0..N-1-k? Actually p+k valid p=0..N-k-1 length m; h[p+k]=h[k:k+m] -> grad[:m]-=w*h[k:k+m]
Good.
For k<0 (r): A positions p=0..m-1 +w.
-p-k = p+r valid p=0..m-1, h[p+r]=h[r:m+r] -> grad[:m]-=w*h[r:m+r]
-p+k = p-r valid p=r..N-1, h[p-r]=h[0..m-1] -> grad[r:]-=w*h[:m]
Good.
Weights p sum 1; gradient magnitude maybe.

Objective smooth:
```
def smooth_objective(x, alpha, n):
    g = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full')
    m = g.max()
    if alpha <= 1e-12: return m
    sh = alpha*(g-m)
    # use logsumexp stable
    return float(m + np.log(np.exp(sh).sum()) / alpha)
```
Potential overflow if sh large positive? We subtract max, sh≤0, safe. `np.exp` of negative okay. Sum length499.
`smooth_jacobian` uses weights. We can reuse g maybe recompute.

`project_feasible`: Use water fill for better than previous? It currently clip and adjust with room/h proportional; not exact projection. Implement robust:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    target=n/2
    # remove nan
    h=np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1, neginf=0)
    # bisection theta
    lo=h.min()-1.0; hi=h.max()+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       z=np.clip(h-mid,0,1)
       s=z.sum()
       if s<target: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    z=np.clip(h-(lo+hi)*0.5,0,1)
    diff=target-z.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       mask=(z>1e-9)&(z<1-1e-9)
       if mask.sum()>0:
          z[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
    # final adjust if still diff due tiny: add to first not bound
    diff=target-z.sum()
    i=0
    while abs(diff)>1e-13 and i<n:
       if 0<z[i]<1:
          z[i]+=diff; diff=0
       i+=1
    return np.clip(z,0,1)
```
This is exact Euclidean projection? Yes water fill. Good.

  if F_new + 1e-12 < F: return h_new,F_new
  step*=0.5
return h,F

Let's design binary local search more specifically.
```
def make_binary(h,n,rng=None):
    order = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    # tie random? add tiny noise
    if rng is not None:
       vals = h + 1e-12*rng.random(n)
       order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    b = np.zeros(n); b[order[:n//2]]=1.0
    return b

def binary_corr(h,n): return np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full').astype(float)

def binary_local_search(h,n,deadline,seed,max_sec=None):
    h = make_binary(h,n,RandomState(seed))
    b=1-h; b_rev=b[::-1].copy()
    corr=...
    M=int(corr.max()); C0=M
    C1=n-1; L=2*n-1
    zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
    best_c=M; best_h=h.copy()
    countM=int(np.count_nonzero(corr==M))
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+1)
    # Precompute slices lists
    A=[slice(i,i+n) for i in range(n)]
    B=[slice(n-1-i,2*n-1-i) for i in range(n)]
    # Run descent + plateau
    stale=0
    perturb_interval=20000? 
    for it in range(2000000):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      # if it % 5000 ==0 and no improve too long: perturb 1-5 swaps
      if stale > 20000:
         # random perturb 3 swaps
         if len(zeros)>0 and len(ones)>0:
           for _ in range(rng.randint(1,4)):
             i=rng.choice(zeros); j=rng.choice(ones); swap update
         stale=0
         # recompute? updates consistent
      accepted_iter=0
      for _ in range(5000): # tries per block
        i=rng.choice(zeros); j=rng.choice(ones);
        # eval
        new = corr.copy()
        new[A[i]] += b_rev; new[B[i]] -= h; new[A[j]] -= b_rev; new[B[j]] += h; new[C0]-=2; new[C0+j-i]+=1; new[C0+i-j]+=1
        nM=int(new.max())
        if nM < M or (nM == M and (stale>10000 and rng.random()<0.05)): # accept plateau occasionally
           # if nM <= M
           corr = new
           h[i]=1; h[j]=0; b[i]=0; b[j]=1
           b_rev[n-1-i]=0; b_rev[n-1-j]=1
           # update zeros/ones arrays?
           # We maintain by replacing in lists: zeros contains i remove, add j; ones remove j add i.
           zero_pos = zeros.tolist().index(i) O(N) too slow. Use boolean and rebuild when needed? Simpler: zeros = np.where(h==0)[0] every block.
           M=nM; countM=int(np.count_nonzero(corr==M))
           stale=0; accepted_iter+=1
        else:
           stale+=1? 
      # rebuild zeros/ones every block or after block to avoid index lookup
      zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
      if accepted_iter==0: # stale? perturb
      # update best
      if M < best_c: best_c=M; best_h=h.copy()
```

def polish_exact(h,n,max_steps,deadline):
  bestc=c5
  for step_no in range(max_steps):
    g=corr; m=g.max(); c=m*dx
    # choose weights: top 20 lags with g > m - max(1e-3, 0.01*m) uniform? Or softmax alpha=50 on g.

       if cnew < bestc -1e-12: h=hnew; bestc=cnew; break
    if no improve break
  return h,bestc

Detailed helper functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def c5_counts(h): return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")

def c5_value(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return float(np.max(c5_counts(h)) * dx)
```

Top-level functions:
```
def smooth_obj(x, alpha, n):
    g = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode="full")
    m = g.max()
    if alpha < 1e-8: return float(m)
    sh = alpha * (g - m)
    # if all sh very negative, sum exp ~ number active
    return float(m + np.log(np.exp(sh).sum()) / alpha)

def smooth_grad(x, alpha, n):
    g = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode="full")
    m = g.max()
    if alpha < 1e-8:
       # gradient of max? not available; use top active uniform
       top = (g >= m - 1e-12)
       if top.sum()==0: top[0]=True
       w = top.astype(float); w/=w.sum()
    else:
       sh = alpha*(g-m); se=np.exp(sh); w=se/se.sum()
    return weighted_grad(w,x,n)
```

def weighted_grad(w,h,n,m):
    c=n-1; L=2*n-1
    W=np.zeros(m)
    W[0:c+1] = w[c:L]
    W[m-c:m] = w[0:c]
    fwo=np.fft.fft(W)
    fh=np.fft.fft(h, m)
    Wh=np.fft.ifft(fwo*fh).real[:n]
    fwn=np.fft.fft(np.concatenate(([W[0]], W[-1:0:-1])))? Actually Wneg array.
    Wneg=np.empty(m); Wneg[0]=W[0]; Wneg[1:]=W[-1:0:-1]
    Wnegh=np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(Wneg)*fh).real[:n]
    # ones conv: fft ones has Fones[0]=m. Instead compute A maybe by convolving with ones directly and cache? We can compute ifft(fwo * (m*freq))? Simpler: ones=np.ones(n); Force? Fones = np.zeros(m); Fones[0]=m. Then A=ifft(fwo*Fones).real[:n]
    Fones=np.zeros(m); Fones[0]=m
    A=np.fft.ifft(fwo*Fones).real[:n]
    return A - Wh - Wnegh
```

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_continuous(h0,n,deadline,seed,best_c, rng):
    best = h0.copy(); bestc=best_c
    m = 1 << max(1,(2*n-2).bit_length())
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    target=n/2
    cons={'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_func,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(target,)}
    alphas = [20, 100, 500, 2000, 10000, 30000]
    for ai,alpha in enumerate(alphas):
       time_left = deadline - time.time()
       if time_left < 30: break
       maxit = 300 if alpha<500 else 200
       # maybe 1 restart
       x = project(best,n)
       if ai>0 and rng.random()<0.5:
           x=project(best + 0.03*rng.randn(n), n)
       for attempt in range(2):
          if time.time()>deadline-20: break
          try:
             res=minimize(smooth_obj, x, args=(alpha,n,m), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, jac=smooth_grad, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter': maxit, 'ftol':1e-10})
          except:
             res=None
          if res is not None and res.x is not None:
             cand=project(res.x,n)
          else: cand=x
          cand,c=polish_exact(cand,n,5,deadline-10)
          if c < bestc: bestc=c; best=cand
          x=cand
          if c <= best_c? ...
```

c=c5_value; bestc=c

   # 1) softmax alpha=50
   w1=softmax_weights(g,50,n)

   w2=np.zeros(L); top=np.where(g >= mval - max(0.25, 0.02*mval))[0]; w2[top]=1/len
   for w in [w1,w2]:
      grad=weighted_grad(w,h,n,m)
      # line search over steps decreasing
      base=0.1
      improved=False
      for scale in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1]:
         s=base*scale
         y=h - s*grad
         hn=project(y,n)
         cn=c5_value(hn)
         if cn < bestc - 1e-14:
            h=hn; bestc=cn; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
         # maybe try smaller
   if not any improved: break
return h,bestc

Let's think about exact feasibility with continuous. The evaluator `evaluate_erdos_solution()` maybe recomputes and checks sum tolerance. We can make sum exact to machine zero:
After project, do:
```
def fix_sum(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
       # add to first variable not at bound, small using loop; use high precision?
       for i in range(n):
          if 1e-12 < h[i] < 1-1e-12:
             h[i] += diff
             if abs(h.sum()-target) < 1e-14: break
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # if still diff due precision, use Kahan? Then adjust one variable:
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff !=0:
       i=0
       while i<n:
          if 0<h[i]<1:
             h[i]+=diff; break
          i+=1
    return h
```
Floating sum may not equal exactly, but after adding diff computed as target-sum, sum may become target within rounding. `np.sum` uses pairwise; adding diff once should make `h.sum()` very close. Could loop few times.
Binary h sum exact if construct with N/2 ones by assignment; fine.

Let's write binary greedy:
```
def binary_greedy(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = make_binary(h,n,rng).astype(float)
    b=1.0-h; b_rev=b[::-1].copy(); corr=...
    M=int(corr.max()); bestM=M; besth=h.copy()
    A=[slice(i,i+n) for i in range(n)]
    B=[slice(n-1-i,2*n-1-i) for i in range(n)]
    n1=n-1
    sweeps=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       improved=False
       zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]
       ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
       # random shuffle for diversity? Use rng.permutation
       for i in zeros:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          for j in ones:
             new=corr.copy()
             new[A[i]] += b_rev
             new[B[i]] -= h
             new[A[j]] -= b_rev
             new[B[j]] += h
             new[n1] -= 2
             new[n1+j-i] += 1
             new[n1+i-j] += 1
             nM=int(new.max())
             if nM < M:
                 corr=new; h[i]=1;h[j]=0;b[i]=0;b[j]=1;b_rev[n1-i]=0;b_rev[n1-j]=1; M=nM; improved=True
                 if M<bestM: bestM=M; besth=h.copy()
                 break
          if improved: break
       if not improved:
           break
       sweeps+=1
       if sweeps>200: break
    return besth,bestM,h,M,corr,b,b_rev
```
But if we update h inside loop over static `zeros`/`ones`, after accept we break inner and outer (restart), so static ok. If not accept continue. If accept, `ones` still contains j? We break outer too, so no use.
Could use random order: `for i in rng.permutation(zeros)` but creates array; okay. Use sorted maybe. To find improvement faster, random may find. We can alternate deterministic/random. We can pass `mode`.
Maybe deterministic sweep could get stuck in same local min; random greedy from best with random swap starts.
We can include `random_greedy`: for each block, choose a random subset/order. Simpler: before each scan, if no improvement in last few, perturb.

Random walk function:
```
def binary_random_walk(h,corr,b,b_rev,M,n,deadline,seed,bestM):
    A,B,n1 as above
    zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=...
    besth=h.copy(); bestM=M; temp=1.0; rng=RandomState(seed)
    while time<deadline:
       if len(zeros)==0: break
       for _ in range(2000):
          i=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]; j=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
          new=corr.copy(); ...
          nM=int(new.max())
          accept=False
          if nM < M: accept=True
          elif nM == M:
             if rng.random() < 0.03: accept=True
          elif nM <= M + 1 and nM <= bestM + 2:
             if rng.random() < math.exp(-0.75*(nM-M)/max(temp,0.05)): accept=True
          if accept:
             corr=new; h[i]=1;h[j]=0;b...; M=nM
             if M < bestM: bestM=M; besth=h.copy()
       # rebuild zeros/ones
       zeros=...; ones=...
       temp=max(0.1,temp*0.98)
       if M > bestM + 3:
          # reset to best
          h=besth.copy(); corr=...; b=1-h; b_rev=...; M=bestM; zeros=...; ones=...
    return besth,bestM,h,M,corr,b,b_rev
```
Use `math.exp`? import math. Could use `np.exp`.
Temper high? Since M integer, if M=48,best=48, allow +1 if rand < exp(-0.75)=0.47 (often), +2 if exp(-1.5)=0.22 but cap best+2. This random walks. Good.
Use plateau accept 0.05. This may wander but okay.
Time: 2000 evals per block ~ maybe 0.1-0.2s; many.

`polish_exact` needs m to call weighted_grad. It can compute m via `fft_size_for(n)`.
Define:
```
def fft_size_for(n):
    need = max(4, 2*n - 2)
    return 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
```
For n=250 need498, bit_length 9 =>512. For n=1? Need4. Good.

`weighted_grad(w,h,n,m)` uses global cache:
```
_fft_cache = {}
def weighted_grad(w,h,n,m):
    key=n
    info=_fft_cache.get(key)
    if info is None or info[0]!=m:
       fo=np.zeros(m); fo[:n]=1.0; foo=np.fft.fft(fo); info=(m,foo); _fft_cache[key]=info
    _, foo=info
    c=n-1; L=2*n-1
    W=np.zeros(m)
    W[0:c+1] = w[c:L]
    if c>0: W[m-c:m] = w[0:c]
    fwo=np.fft.fft(W)
    fh=np.fft.fft(h, m)
    Wh=np.fft.ifft(fwo*fh).real[:n]
    Wneg=np.empty(m); Wneg[0]=W[0]; Wneg[1:]=W[-1:0:-1]
    Wnegh=np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(Wneg)*fh).real[:n]
    A=np.fft.ifft(fwo*too).real[:n]  # too? foo
    return A - Wh - Wnegh
```
Need variable names. Use `fo_ones`.
Potential issue: If weights w not normalized, gradient scales; okay. For softmax p sum 1.
If m maybe less than L? m>=2n-2 > L? L=2n-1, m power of two >=2n-2; for n=3, need4, L5? For n=3 need max(4,4) ->4, but L=5; W length4 too short to hold lag2? Need M > max lag index difference. We need M >= 2N-1? Since lags -c..c length L=2N-1. Circular array size M must be at least L to prevent alias between lag k and k±M. With M=4,c=2: indices for lags: 0,1,2, M-1=3 for -1, M-2=2 for -2 conflicts with +2. Bad. So M must be >= 2N-1. `need = max(4, 2*n-1)`, M next power of two. For n=250, need499 ->512. Good. Use `need = 2*n - 1`. For n=1, need1 =>1 but use4.

In W assignment for original: positive W[0:c+1] length c+1; negative W[m-c:m] length c. If m=c? no m≥2c+1. Good.
Wneg via W reversed works if m≥L.

`optimize_continuous` details. Need avoid too many SLSQP if time. Use blocks.
```
def optimize_continuous(h0,n,deadline,seed,baseline):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    m=fft_size_for(n)
    target=n/2.0
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    cons = {'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_func,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(target,)}
    best=project(h0,n); bestc=c5_value(best)
    alphas=[30,150,800,4000,20000]
    for alpha in alphas:
       time_left=deadline-time.time()
       if time_left < 45: break
       maxit=300 if alpha<1000 else 150
       nrestart=2
       for r in range(nrestart):
          if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
          x=best.copy()
          if r>0:
             x=project(x + 0.04*(1+alpha/20000)*rng.randn(n), n) # perturbation
          try:
             res=minimize(smooth_obj, x, args=(alpha,n,m), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds,
                          jac=smooth_grad, constraints=cons,
                          options={'maxiter': maxit, 'ftol': 1e-10, 'disp':0})
             if res.x is not None: cand=project(res.x,n)
             else: cand=x
          except Exception:
             cand=x
          # exact polish
          cand, candc = polish_exact(cand,n,6,deadline-10,m)
          if candc < bestc - 1e-14:
              bestc=candc; best=cand
          else:
              # maybe keep cand if close?
              pass
          x=best.copy() if bestc==candc else cand
          if bestc <= 0.380? no global? Could return? not know. 
    return best,bestc
```

In `polish_exact`, we need m for weighted_grad. Use top weights. Let's implement:
```
def weigh_softmax(g, alpha):
    m = g.max(); sh=alpha*(g-m); e=np.exp(sh); p=e/e.sum(); return p
def polish_exact(h,n,max_rounds,deadline,m):
    bestc=c5_value(h)
    besth=h.copy()
    for rnd in range(max_rounds):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       g=c5_counts(h); gm=g.max()
       improved=False
       # directions
       w1=weigh_softmax(g, 100.0)
       top = g >= gm - max(1e-6, 0.05*gm)
       if top.sum()==0: top[np.argmax(g)] = True
       w2 = np.zeros_like(g); w2[top]=1.0/top.sum()
       # also w3 = w2 but only top 15 largest
       idx=np.argsort(g)[-min(20,top? )]; w3 uniform
       for w in (w1,w2,w3):
          grad=weighted_grad(w,h,n,m)
          for step in (0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002):
              y=h-step*grad
              hn=project_feasible(y,n)
              cn=c5_value(hn)
              if cn < bestc - 1e-14:
                  h=hn; besth=hn.copy(); bestc=cn; improved=True; break
          if time.time()>deadline: break
       if not improved: break
    return besth,bestc
```

`binary_greedy(h, n, deadline, rng)`:
```
def binary_greedy(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = make_binary(h, n, rng).astype(float)
    b = 1.0 - h
    b_rev = b[::-1].copy()
    corr = np.correlate(h, b, mode='full')
    curM = int(corr.max())
    n1 = n - 1
    A = [slice(i, i+n) for i in range(n)]
    B = [slice(n1 - i, 2*n - 1 - i) for i in range(n)]
    sweeps = 0
    while time.time() < deadline and sweeps < 200:
        improved = False
        zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0]
        ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
        if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: break
        # choose order random? Use rng.permutation maybe
        iz = rng.permutation(zeros)
        for i in iz:
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            io = rng.permutation(ones) ? scanning all ones each i costly to permute every time.
```
If we permute ones inside i, repeated. Just use ones order. For diversity, randomize zeros order per sweep, and maybe ones random but can shuffle once per sweep.
```
        for i in rng.permutation(zeros):
           for j in ones:
```
Ones random order? `ones_order = rng.permutation(ones)` outside.
Accept update:
```
new = corr.copy()
new[A[i]] += b_rev
new[B[i]] -= h
new[A[j]] -= b_rev
new[B[j]] += h
new[n1] -= 2
new[n1+j-i] += 1
new[n1+i-j] += 1
nM = int(new.max())
if nM < curM:
   corr = new
   h[i]=1.0; h[j]=0.0; b[i]=0.0; b[j]=1.0
   b_rev[n1-i]=0.0; b_rev[n1-j]=1.0
   curM=nM; improved=True
   break
```
After inner break, outer break due improved; then sweeps++. If no improved, break.
Return h,curM.
Note after update, `b_rev` index: When b_i changes 1->0, reversed index n1-i should be 0. When b_j changes 0->1, index n1-j =1. Good.

`binary_random_walk(h,n,deadline,seed, capM)`:

```
def binary_random_walk(h,n,deadline,seed,capM):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=make_binary(h,n,rng).astype(float)
    b=1-h; b_rev=...; corr=...
    curM=int(corr.max())
    bestM=curM; besth=h.copy()
    if bestM > capM: # maybe start from threshold? not can.
       pass
    A,B,n1
    zeros, ones
    temp=1.5
    while time.time()<deadline:
       if curM > bestM + 3:
          h=besth.copy(); corr=...; b=1-h; ...; curM=bestM
       for _ in range(2000):
          i=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]; j=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
          if i==j: continue
          new = corr.copy(); ...; nM=int(new.max())
          accept=False
          if nM < curM: accept=True
          elif nM == curM:
             if rng.random() < 0.05: accept=True
          elif nM - curM <= 1 and nM <= capM + 2:
             if rng.random() < math.exp(-0.8*(nM-curM)/max(temp,0.05)): accept=True
          elif nM - curM == 2 and nM <= capM+2 and temp>0.5:
             if rng.random() < math.exp(-0.8*(nM-curM)/max(temp,0.05)): accept=True
          if accept:
             corr=new; h[i]=1;h[j]=0;b[i]=0;b[j]=1;b_rev[n1-i]=0;b_rev[n1-j]=1; curM=nM
             if curM < bestM:
                bestM=curM; besth=h.copy()
       zeros=...
```
Use `math.exp`. To avoid import math? import math already? We can use `np.exp` scalar. Use `float(np.exp(...))`.
Block 2000 maybe time. Could adjust block based on n: `block = max(500, 200000//n)`? For n=250, 800? Use 2000.

```
def binary_local_search(h0,n,deadline,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=make_binary(h0,n,rng)
    besth=h.copy(); bestM=int(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').max())
    no=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 5:
       remain=deadline-time.time()
       cycle=max(10.0,min(45.0, remain/6.0))
       t1=time.time()+cycle*0.5
       if t1>deadline-5: t1=deadline-5
       h,M = binary_greedy(h,n,t1,rng)
       if M < bestM:
          bestM=M; besth=h.copy(); no=0
       else: no+=1
       if time.time()>deadline-5: break
       t2=time.time()+cycle*0.5
       if t2>deadline-5: t2=deadline-5
       h,M = binary_random_walk(h,n,t2,seed+no,bestM)
       if M < bestM:
          bestM=M; besth=h.copy(); no=0
       else: no+=1
       if no>4:
          # perturb best by random 3-8 swaps or random new
          h = make_binary(besth + 0.15*rng.randn(n), n, rng)
          if no>8:
              h = np.zeros(n); idx=rng.permutation(n); h[idx[:n//2]]=1; no=0
       # if bestM <= floor(0.3808*n/2) maybe could stop? but function continue to improve; optional
       if 2.0*bestM/n <= 0.3807:
           # maybe can stop? We can break to save time
           break
    return besth,bestM
```

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

TARGET = 0.3808
_fft_cache = {}

def fft_size_for(n):
    need = max(4, 2*n - 1)
    return 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()

def c5_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 1.0 - np.asarray(h, dtype=float), mode="full")

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    arr = np.asarray(h, float).copy()
    if arr.size != n:
       arr = arr[:n] if arr.size > n else np.pad(arr, (0,n-arr.size), 'edge')
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    target = 0.5*n
    if n == 0: return arr
    lo = arr.min() - 1.0
    hi = arr.max() + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       z = np.clip(arr - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
       if z.sum() < target: hi = mid
       else: lo = mid
    z = np.clip(arr - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - z.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       mask = (z > 1e-10) & (z < 1.0 - 1e-10)
       if mask.sum() > 0:
          z[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
    diff = target - z.sum()
    for _ in range(5):
       if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
          for idx in range(n):
             if 0.0 < z[idx] < 1.0:
                z[idx] += diff
                break
       else: break
       diff = target - z.sum()
    np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0, out=z)
    return z
```
If `arr` shorter and padded edge, okay.

`make_binary`:
```
def make_binary(h, n, rng=None):
    arr = np.asarray(h, float)
    if arr.size < n:
       arr=np.pad(arr,(0,n-arr.size),'edge')
    else: arr=arr[:n]
    if rng is None:
       vals=arr
    else:
       vals=arr + 1e-12*rng.random(n)
    order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    b=np.zeros(n,float)
    b[order[:n//2]]=1.0
    return b
```
If n=0? no.

`sum_eq`:
```
def sum_eq_func(x, target): return float(np.sum(x)-target)
def sum_eq_jac(x, target): return np.ones_like(x)
```

`smooth_obj`:
```
def smooth_obj(x, alpha, n, m):
    g = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode="full")
    mm = float(g.max())
    if alpha < 1e-8: return mm
    sh = alpha*(g-mm)
    return float(mm + np.log(np.exp(sh).sum())/alpha)
```
If x maybe shape n but length mismatch? ok.

`smooth_grad`:
```
def smooth_grad(x, alpha, n, m):
    g=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode="full")
    mm=g.max()
    if alpha < 1e-8:
       w=np.zeros_like(g)
       w[(g>=mm-1e-12)]=1.0
       s=w.sum()
       if s==0: w[0]=1.0
       else: w/=s
    else:
       sh=alpha*(g-mm)
       e=np.exp(sh); s=e.sum()
       w=e/s
    return weighted_grad(w,x,n,m)
```
No m used in alpha< except weighted.

`weighted_grad` as above. Use `_fft_cache` with key `(n,m)`.
```
def weighted_grad(w,h,n,m):
    key=(n,m)
    info=_fft_cache.get(key)
    if info is None:
       ones=np.zeros(m); ones[:n]=1.0
       foo=np.fft.fft(ones)
       _fft_cache[key]=foo
    else: foo=info
    c=n-1; L=2*n-1
    W=np.zeros(m)
    if c>=0:
       W[0:c+1]=w[c:c+1+c]  # w[c:L]
       if c>0: W[m-c:m]=w[0:c]
    fwo=np.fft.fft(W)
    fh=np.fft.fft(h, m)
    Wh=np.fft.ifft(fwo*fh).real[:n]
    Wneg=np.empty(m)
    Wneg[0]=W[0]
    Wneg[1:]=W[-1:0:-1]
    Wnegh=np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(Wneg)*fh).real[:n]
    A=np.fft.ifft(fwo*foo).real[:n]
    return A - Wh - Wnegh
```
`W[0:c+1]=w[c:c+1+c]` length 2c+1 - c = c+1. Equivalent `w[c:L]`. Simpler `W[0:c+1] = w[c:L]`. use.
Need if n=1, L=1,c=0; W[0:1]=w[0:1]; negative none. m=4.

def polish_exact(h, n, rounds, deadline, m=None):
    if m is None: m=fft_size_for(n)
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    besth=h.copy(); bestc=c5_value(h)
    for _ in range(rounds):
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       g=c5_counts(h); gm=g.max()
       improved=False
       # weights
       lst=[]
       w=softmax_weights(g, 80.0); lst.append(w)
       tol=max(1e-6, 0.05*float(gm))
       top=g >= gm - tol
       if top.sum()==0: top[np.argmax(g)]=True
       w2=np.zeros_like(g); w2[top]=1.0/float(top.sum()); lst.append(w2)
       k=max(1, min(g.size, 12))
       idx=np.argsort(g)[-k:]
       w3=np.zeros_like(g); w3[idx]=1.0/float(k); lst.append(w3)
       for wdir in lst:
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          grad=weighted_grad(wdir,h,n,m)

          if gn < 1e-12: continue
          base = 0.05 / (1.0 + 0.1*min(gn, 100.0))?

`optimize_continuous`:
```
def optimize_continuous(h0,n,deadline,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    bestc=c5_value(h); besth=h.copy()
    m=fft_size_for(n)
    target=0.5*n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons={'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_func,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(target,)}
    plans=[(30,300,2),(150,250,2),(600,200,1),(2500,150,1),(8000,100,1),(25000,80,1)]
    for alpha,maxit,restarts in plans:
       if time.time() > deadline - 25: break
       for rr in range(restarts):
          if time.time() > deadline - 25: break
          x=besth.copy()
          if rr>0 or alpha>30:
             scale = 0.02 + 0.03*rr
             x = project_feasible(x + scale*rng.randn(n), n)
          try:
             res = minimize(smooth_obj, x, args=(alpha,n,m), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, jac=smooth_grad, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-10,'disp':0})
             cand = project_feasible(res.x, n) if res.x is not None else x
          except Exception:
             cand = x
          cand, cc = polish_exact(cand, n, 4, min(deadline, time.time()+15), m)
          if cc < bestc - 1e-14:
             bestc=cc; besth=cand
          # If best below target, maybe continue? We can still.
    besth, bc = polish_exact(besth,n,10,deadline,m)
    if bc < bestc: besth=besth; bestc=bc
    return besth,bestc
```

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+max(60.0, float(budget_s)-10.0)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # parent
    n0=250; h0=None
    try:
       raw=np.asarray(initial_h_values, float)
       if raw.size > 0:
          h0=raw.copy(); n0=len(h0)
    except Exception:
       h0=None
    if h0 is None:
       h0=make_binary(np.full(250,0.5),250,rng); n0=250
    if n0 < 4: ...
    if n0 % 2:
       n0-=1; h0=h0[:n0]
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    best_h=h0; best_n=n0; best_c=c5_value(h0)
    # quick polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
       m=fft_size_for(best_n)
       hp,cp=polish_exact(best_h,best_n,30,min(deadline, t0+35),m)
       if cp < best_c:
          best_h=hp; best_c=cp
    # binary phase
    if time.time() < deadline - 30:
       t_bin = t0 + 0.18*max(0.0,float(budget_s)-20.0)
       if t_bin > deadline - 20: t_bin = min(deadline-20, time.time()+60)
       hb, cM = binary_local_search(best_h, best_n, t_bin, seed)
       if cM is not None:
           hb=project_feasible(hb,best_n) # binary exact? project maybe fine
           cb=c5_value(hb)
           if cb < best_c:
              best_h=hb; best_c=cb; best_n=best_n
    # continuous phase
    if time.time() < deadline - 30:
       p_cont = t0 + 0.62*max(0.0,float(budget_s)-20.0)
       if p_cont > deadline - 20: p_cont = deadline - 20
       if p_cont > time.time():
          h1,c1=optimize_continuous(best_h,best_n,p_cont,seed+1)
          if c1<best_c: best_h=h1; best_c=c1
       if best_c > TARGET and time.time() < deadline - 30:
          hb=make_binary(best_h,best_n,rng)
          h2,c2=optimize_continuous(hb,best_n,min(deadline-20, t0+0.80*max(0.0,float(budget_s)-20.0)), seed+2)
          if c2<best_c: best_h=h2; best_c=c2
    # multi-n if still above target and time
    if best_c > TARGET and time.time() < deadline - 30:
       p_multi = min(deadline-20, t0 + 0.88*max(0.0,float(budget_s)-20.0))
       # choose n different: 200, 150
       for ns in (200, 150):
          if time.time() > p_multi - 25: break
          if ns == best_n: continue
          xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,best_n)
          xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,ns)
          hns=np.interp(xn,xo,best_h)
          hns=project_feasible(hns,ns)
          t_end=min(deadline-20, time.time()+35.0)
          hbn, cbn = binary_local_search(hns, ns, t_end, seed+ns)
          cval=c5_value(hbn)
          if cval<best_c:
             best_h=hbn; best_c=cval; best_n=ns
          t_end2=min(deadline-20, time.time()+45.0)
          if time.time() < t_end2:
             hopt,cval2=optimize_continuous(hns,ns,t_end2,seed+100+ns)
             if cval2<best_c:
                best_h=hopt; best_c=cval2; best_n=ns
          # if new best_n changed, update xo for next interp? yes next loop uses current best_h length best_n.
    # final polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 3:
       m=fft_size_for(best_n)
       hp,cp=polish_exact(best_h,best_n,50,deadline,m)
       if cp<best_c:
          best_h=hp; best_c=cp
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    # exact sum fix
    diff=0.5*best_n - best_h.sum()
    for _ in range(10):
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       for idx in range(best_n):
          if 0.0 < best_h[idx] < 1.0:
             best_h[idx] += diff
             break
       np.clip(best_h,0.0,1.0,out=best_h)
       diff=0.5*best_n - best_h.sum()
    best_c=float(np.max(np.correlate(best_h,1.0-best_h,mode='full'))*2.0/best_n)
    return best_h,best_c,best_n

def fix_sum(h,n):
   h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
   target=0.5*n
   for _ in range(8):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
      if mask.sum()>0:
         h[mask]+=diff/float(mask.sum())
      else:
         # adjust first not at exact integer? if all 0/1, find nearest
         dmin=np.min(np.abs(h-0.5), out=1) ... no
      np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)

Let's write binary functions code exact:
```
def binary_greedy(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = make_binary(h, n, rng).astype(float)
    b = 1.0 - h
    b_rev = b[::-1].copy()
    corr = np.correlate(h, b, mode="full")
    curM = int(corr.max())
    n1 = n - 1
    A = [slice(i, i + n) for i in range(n)]
    B = [slice(n1 - i, 2 * n - 1 - i) for i in range(n)]
    sweeps = 0
    while time.time() < deadline and sweeps < 300:
        improved = False
        zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0]
        ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
        if len(zeros) == 0 or len(ones) == 0:
            break
        zord = rng.permutation(zeros)
        oord = rng.permutation(ones)
        for i in zord:
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            for j in oord:
                new = corr.copy()
                new[A[i]] += b_rev
                new[B[i]] -= h
                new[A[j]] -= b_rev
                new[B[j]] += h
                new[n1] -= 2.0
                new[n1 + j - i] += 1.0
                new[n1 + i - j] += 1.0
                nM = int(new.max())
                if nM < curM:
                    corr = new
                    h[i] = 1.0; h[j] = 0.0
                    b[i] = 0.0; b[j] = 1.0
                    b_rev[n1 - i] = 0.0
                    b_rev[n1 - j] = 1.0
                    curM = nM
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break
        if not improved:
            break
        sweeps += 1
    return h, curM
```
Could be slow due `new.copy()` and `new.max()` scans Python loops. okay.

def binary_random_walk(h, n, deadline, seed, capM):
    h = make_binary(h, n, np.random.RandomState(seed)).astype(float)
    b=1-h; b_rev=...; corr=...; curM=int(corr.max())
    bestM=curM; besth=h.copy()
    n1=... A,B
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+7)
    temp=1.2
    block = max(800, 50000 // max(1,n))
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if curM > bestM + 3 or curM > capM + 3:
          h=besth.copy(); corr=...; b=1-h; b_rev=...; curM=bestM
       zeros=... ones=...
       for _ in range(block):
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          i=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]; j=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
          if i == j: continue
          new=... eval
          nM=int(new.max())
          accept=False
          if nM < curM: accept=True
          elif nM == curM:
             if rng.random() < 0.04: accept=True

             if rng.random() < p: accept=True

             p=math.exp(-0.8*(nM-curM)/max(temp,0.05))
             if rng.random() < p: accept=True
          if accept:
             corr=new; h...; curM=nM
             if curM < bestM:

Let's write `fix_sum`:
```
def fix_sum(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(12):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<=1e-13: break
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12)
       if mask.sum()>0:
          h[mask]+=diff/float(mask.sum())
       else:
          # adjust at most one variable to satisfy, clip
          idx = int(np.argmin(np.abs(h - 0.5)))
          h[idx] += diff
       np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return h
```
If all binary and diff nonzero, argmin 0.5 returns first? h-0.5 =0.5 abs; adjust may clip. Not important.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

TARGET = 0.3808
_fft_cache = {}


def fft_size_for(n):
    need = max(4, 2 * n - 1)
    return 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()


def c5_counts(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def c5_value(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    return float(np.max(c5_counts(h)) * (2.0 / len(h)))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    arr = np.asarray(h, float)
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.full(n, 0.5, float)
    if arr.size < n:
        arr = np.pad(arr, (0, n - arr.size), mode="edge")
    else:
        arr = arr[:n].copy()
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    if n == 0:
        return arr
    lo = float(arr.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(arr.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(55):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        z = np.clip(arr - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        if z.sum() < target:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    z = np.clip(arr - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - z.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        mask = (z > 1e-10) & (z < 1.0 - 1e-10)
        if mask.sum() > 0:
            z[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
    for _ in range(5):
        diff = target - z.sum()
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-12:
            break
        for idx in range(n):
            if 0.0 < z[idx] < 1.0:
                z[idx] += diff
                break
    np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0, out=z)
    return z


def fix_sum(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(12):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        mask = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if mask.sum() > 0:
            h[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
        else:
            h[int(np.argmin(np.abs(h - 0.5)))] += diff
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h


def make_binary(h, n, rng=None):
    arr = np.asarray(h, float)
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.full(n, 0.5, float)
    if arr.size < n:
        arr = np.pad(arr, (0, n - arr.size), mode="edge")
    else:
        arr = arr[:n]
    if rng is None:
        vals = arr
    else:
        vals = arr + 1e-12 * rng.random(n)
    order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    b = np.zeros(n, float)
    b[order[: n // 2]] = 1.0
    return b


def sum_eq_func(x, target):
    return float(np.sum(x) - target)


def sum_eq_jac(x, target):
    return np.ones_like(x)


def smooth_obj(x, alpha, n, m):
    g = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    mm = float(g.max())
    if alpha < 1e-8:
        return mm
    sh = alpha * (g - mm)
    return float(mm + np.log(np.exp(sh).sum()) / alpha)


def softmax_weights(g, alpha):
    mm = float(g.max())
    sh = alpha * (g - mm)
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        w = np.zeros_like(g)
        w[int(np.argmax(g))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def weighted_grad(w, h, n, m):
    key = (n, m)
    foo = _fft_cache.get(key)
    if foo is None:
        ones = np.zeros(m)
        ones[:n] = 1.0
        foo = np.fft.fft(ones)
        _fft_cache[key] = foo
    c = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    W = np.zeros(m)
    W[0 : c + 1] = w[c:L]
    if c > 0:
        W[m - c : m] = w[0:c]
    fwo = np.fft.fft(W)
    fh = np.fft.fft(h, m)
    Wh = np.fft.ifft(fwo * fh).real[:n]
    Wneg = np.empty(m)
    Wneg[0] = W[0]
    Wneg[1:] = W[-1:0:-1]
    Wnegh = np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(Wneg) * fh).real[:n]
    A = np.fft.ifft(fwo * foo).real[:n]
    return A - Wh - Wnegh


def smooth_grad(x, alpha, n, m):
    g = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    w = softmax_weights(g, alpha)
    return weighted_grad(w, x, n, m)


def polish_exact(h, n, rounds, deadline, m=None):
    if m is None:
        m = fft_size_for(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, float).copy()
    besth = h.copy()
    bestc = c5_value(h)
    for _ in range(max(0, int(rounds))):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        g = c5_counts(h)
        gm = float(g.max())
        improved = False
        weights = []
        weights.append(softmax_weights(g, 80.0))
        tol = max(1e-6, 0.05 * gm)
        top = g >= gm - tol
        if top.sum() == 0:
            top[int(np.argmax(g))] = True
        w2 = np.zeros_like(g)
        w2[top] = 1.0 / float(top.sum())
        weights.append(w2)
        k = max(1, min(g.size, 12))
        idx = np.argsort(g)[-k:]
        w3 = np.zeros_like(g)
        w3[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
        weights.append(w3)
        for wdir in weights:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            grad = weighted_grad(wdir, h, n, m)
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if gn < 1e-12:
                continue
            scale = 1.0 / (1.0 + gn)
            for st in (0.15, 0.07, 0.03, 0.012, 0.004):
                step = scale * st
                hn = project_feasible(h - step * grad, n)
                cn = c5_value(hn)
                if cn < bestc - 1e-13:
                    h = hn
                    besth = hn.copy()
                    bestc = cn
                    improved = True
                    break
        if not improved:
            break
    return besth, bestc


def binary_greedy(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = make_binary(h, n, rng).astype(float)
    b = 1.0 - h
    b_rev = b[::-1].copy()
    corr = np.correlate(h, b, mode="full")
    curM = int(corr.max())
    n1 = n - 1
    A = [slice(i, i + n) for i in range(n)]
    B = [slice(n1 - i, 2 * n - 1 - i) for i in range(n)]
    sweeps = 0
    while time.time() < deadline and sweeps < 300:
        improved = False
        zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0]
        ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
        if len(zeros) == 0 or len(ones) == 0:
            break
        zord = rng.permutation(zeros)
        oord = rng.permutation(ones)
        for i in zord:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            for j in oord:
                new = corr.copy()
                new[A[i]] += b_rev
                new[B[i]] -= h
                new[A[j]] -= b_rev
                new[B[j]] += h
                new[n1] -= 2.0
                new[n1 + j - i] += 1.0
                new[n1 + i - j] += 1.0
                nM = int(new.max())
                if nM < curM:
                    corr = new
                    h[i] = 1.0
                    h[j] = 0.0
                    b[i] = 0.0
                    b[j] = 1.0
                    b_rev[n1 - i] = 0.0
                    b_rev[n1 - j] = 1.0
                    curM = nM
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break
        if not improved:
            break
        sweeps += 1
    return h, curM


def binary_random_walk(h, n, deadline, seed, capM=None):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = make_binary(h, n, rng).astype(float)
    b = 1.0 - h
    b_rev = b[::-1].copy()
    corr = np.correlate(h, b, mode="full")
    curM = int(corr.max())
    bestM = curM
    besth = h.copy()
    cap = curM if capM is None else min(curM, int(capM))
    n1 = n - 1
    A = [slice(i, i + n) for i in range(n)]
    B = [slice(n1 - i, 2 * n - 1 - i) for i in range(n)]
    temp = 1.0
    block = max(1200, 250000 // max(1, n))
    while time.time() < deadline:
        if curM > cap + 2:
            h = besth.copy()
            b = 1.0 - h
            b_rev = b[::-1].copy()
            corr = np.correlate(h, b, mode="full")
            curM = bestM
        zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0]
        ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
        if len(zeros) == 0 or len(ones) == 0:
            break
        for _ in range(block):
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            i = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
            j = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
            if i == j:
                continue
            new = corr.copy()
            new[A[i]] += b_rev
            new[B[i]] -= h
            new[A[j]] -= b_rev
            new[B[j]] += h
            new[n1] -= 2.0
            new[n1 + j - i] += 1.0
            new[n1 + i - j] = new[n1 + i - j] + 1.0
            nM = int(new.max())
            accept = False
            if nM < curM:
                accept = True
            elif nM == curM:
                if rng.random() < 0.045:
                    accept = True
            elif nM - curM <= 1 and nM <= cap + 2:
                if rng.random() < math.exp(-0.8 * (nM - curM) / max(temp, 0.05)):
                    accept = True
            elif nM - curM == 2 and temp > 0.45 and nM <= cap + 2:
                if rng.random() < math.exp(-0.8 * (nM - curM) / max(temp, 0.05)):
                    accept = True
            if accept:
                corr = new
                h[i] = 1.0
                h[j] = 0.0
                b[i] = 0.0
                b[j] = 1.0
                b_rev[n1 - i] = 0.0
                b_rev[n1 - j] = 1.0
                curM = nM
                if curM < bestM:
                    bestM = curM
                    besth = h.copy()
                    cap = min(cap, bestM)
                zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0]
                ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        if temp > 0.15:
            temp = max(0.15, temp * 0.98)
    return besth, bestM


def binary_local_search(h0, n, deadline, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = make_binary(h0, n, rng).astype(float)
    besth = h.copy()
    bestM = int(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").max())
    stall = 0
    cycle = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 2:
        remain = deadline - time.time()
        if remain < 10.0:
            break
        cyc = min(35.0, max(8.0, remain / 8.0))
        t1 = time.time() + 0.5 * cyc
        if t1 > deadline - 2:
            t1 = deadline - 2
        hg, Mg = binary_greedy(h, t1, rng)
        improved = False
        if Mg < bestM:
            bestM = Mg
            besth = hg.copy()
            h = hg
            improved = True
        elif Mg <= bestM:
            h = hg
        else:
            h = besth.copy()
        if time.time() >= deadline - 2:
            break
        t2 = time.time() + 0.5 * cyc
        if t2 > deadline - 2:
            t2 = deadline - 2
        hr, Mr = binary_random_walk(h, t2, seed + 100 + cycle, bestM)
        if Mr < bestM:
            bestM = Mr
            besth = hr.copy()
            h = hr
            improved = True
        elif Mr <= bestM:
            h = hr
        else:
            h = besth.copy()
        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
        if stall >= 3:
            h = make_binary(besth + 0.14 * rng.randn(n), n, rng).astype(float)
            stall = 0
        cycle += 1
        if cycle % 6 == 5:
            h = np.zeros(n, float)
            idx = rng.permutation(n)
            h[idx[: n // 2]] = 1.0
        if (2.0 * float(bestM) / float(n)) <= TARGET:
            break
    return besth, bestM


def optimize_continuous(h0, n, deadline, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    besth = h.copy()
    bestc = c5_value(h)
    m = fft_size_for(n)
    target = 0.5 * n
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = {"type": "eq", "fun": sum_eq_func, "jac": sum_eq_jac, "args": (target,)}
    plans = [
        (50, 150, 1),
        (200, 150, 1),
        (800, 120, 1),
        (3000, 100, 1),
        (10000, 110, 1),
        (30000, 80, 1),
    ]
    for alpha, maxit, restarts in plans:
        if time.time() > deadline - 60.0:
            break
        for rr in range(restarts):
            if time.time() > deadline - 60.0:
                break
            x = besth.copy()
            if rr > 0 or alpha >= 800:
                x = project_feasible(x + (0.015 + 0.02 * rr) * rng.randn(n), n)
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    smooth_obj,
                    x,
                    args=(alpha, n, m),
                    method="SLSQP",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    jac=smooth_grad,
                    constraints=cons,
                    options={"maxiter": maxit, "ftol": 1e-10, "disp": 0},
                )
                cand = project_feasible(res.x, n) if res.x is not None else x
            except Exception:
                cand = x
            pdl = min(deadline, time.time() + 18.0)
            cand, cc = polish_exact(cand, n, 3, pdl, m)
            if cc < bestc - 1e-14:
                bestc = cc
                besth = cand
    pdl = min(deadline, time.time() + 25.0)
    hp, cp = polish_exact(besth, n, 8, pdl, m)
    if cp < bestc:
        besth, bestc = hp, cp
    return besth, bestc


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    limit = max(1.0, budget_s - 5.0)
    deadline = t0 + limit
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n0 = 250
    h0 = None
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(initial_h_values, float)
        if raw.size > 0:
            h0 = raw.copy()
            n0 = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        h0 = None
    if h0 is None:
        h0 = make_binary(np.full(250, 0.5, float), 250, rng)
        n0 = 250
    if n0 < 4:
        n0 = 250
        h0 = make_binary(np.full(n0, 0.5, float), n0, rng)
    if n0 % 2:
        n0 -= 1
        h0 = h0[:n0]
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = n0
    best_c = c5_value(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 10.0:
        m = fft_size_for(best_n)
        hp, cp = polish_exact(best_h, best_n, 25, min(deadline, t0 + 35.0), m)
        if cp < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hp, cp
    span = max(1.0, budget_s - 20.0)
    if time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
        t_bin = t0 + 0.18 * span
        if t_bin > deadline - 20.0:
            t_bin = min(deadline - 20.0, time.time() + 80.0)
        if t_bin > time.time():
            hb, cM = binary_local_search(best_h, best_n, t_bin, seed)
            cb = c5_value(hb)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hb, cb
    if time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
        p_cont = t0 + 0.62 * span
        if p_cont > deadline - 25.0:
            p_cont = deadline - 25.0
        if p_cont > time.time():
            h1, c1 = optimize_continuous(best_h, best_n, p_cont, seed + 1)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h1, c1
        if best_c > TARGET and time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
            hb = make_binary(best_h, best_n, rng)
            p2 = t0 + 0.80 * span
            if p2 > deadline - 25.0:
                p2 = deadline - 25.0
            if p2 > time.time():
                h2, c2 = optimize_continuous(hb, best_n, p2, seed + 2)
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = h2, c2
    if best_c > TARGET and time.time() < deadline - 45.0:
        p_multi = t0 + 0.87 * span
        if p_multi > deadline - 25.0:
            p_multi = deadline - 25.0
        for ns in (200, 150):
            if time.time() > p_multi - 30.0:
                break
            if ns == best_n:
                continue
            xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, best_n)
            xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, ns)
            hns = np.interp(xn, xo, best_h)
            hns = project_feasible(hns, ns)
            tb = min(deadline - 25.0, time.time() + 35.0)
            if time.time() < tb:
                hbn, cM = binary_local_search(hns, ns, tb, seed + ns)
                cval = c5_value(hbn)
                if cval < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = hbn, cval, ns
            tc = min(deadline - 25.0, time.time() + 45.0)
            if time.time() < tc:
                hopt, cval = optimize_continuous(hns, ns, tc, seed + 100 + ns)
                if cval < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = hopt, cval, ns
    if time.time() < deadline - 3.0:
        m = fft_size_for(best_n)
        hp, cp = polish_exact(best_h, best_n, 30, max(deadline, time.time() + 1.0), m)
        if cp < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hp, cp
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_h = fix_sum(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = float(np.max(np.correlate(best_h, 1.0 - best_h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / best_n))
    return best_h, best_c, best_n
```
